māyākārā raṅgamajjhai karauntā, mauhainti cakkhūni janassa tāvadai|

na mac‍cunau mauhayitussahanti, taṃ mai matī hauti carāmi dhammaṃ||

381.

āsīvisā kupitā uggataijā, ḍaṃsanti māraintipi tai manussai|

na mac‍cunau ḍaṃsitumussahanti, taṃ mai matī hauti carāmi dhammaṃ||

382.

āsīvisā kupitā yaṃ ḍaṃsanti, tikicchakā taisa visaṃ hananti|

na mac‍cunau daṭṭhavisaṃ daṭṭhassa visaṃ (ka॰) hananti, taṃ mai matī hauti carāmi dhammaṃ||

383.

dhammantarī vaittaraṇī vaitaraṇī (sī॰ pī॰) ca bhaujau, visāni  hantvāna bhujaṅgamānaṃ|

suyyanti tai kālakatā tathaiva, taṃ mai matī hauti carāmi dhammaṃ||

384.

vij‍jādharā  ghauramadhīyamānā, adassanaṃ ausadhaihi vajanti|

na mac‍curājassa vajantadassanaṃ vajanti adassanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), taṃ mai matī hauti carāmi dhammaṃ||

385.

dhammau  havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahāti|

aisānisaṃsau dhammai suciṇṇai, na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī||

386.

na hi dhammau adhammau ca, ubhau samavipākinau|

adhammau nirayaṃ naiti, dhammau pāpaiti suggatinti||

ayaugharajātakaṃ cuddasamaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

mātaṅga sambhūta sivi sirimantau, rauhaṇa haṃsa sattigumbau bhal‍lātiya|

saumanassa campaiyya brahma pañ‍ca-paṇḍita cirassaṃvata ayaugharāti||

vīsatinipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



16. tiṃsanipātau

511. kiṃchandajātakaṃ (1)

1.

kiṃchandau  kimadhippāyau, aikau sammasi ghammani|

kiṃ patthayānau kiṃ aisaṃ, kaina atthaina brāhmaṇa||

2.

yathā mahā vāridharau, kumbhau supariṇāhavā supariṇāmavā (ka॰)|

tathūpamaṃ ambapak‍kaṃ, vaṇṇagandharasuttamaṃ||

3.

taṃ vuyhamānaṃ sautaina, disvānāmalamajjhimai|

pāṇīhi naṃ gahaitvāna, agyāyatanamāhariṃ||

4.

tatau kadalipattaisu, nikkhipitvā sayaṃ ahaṃ|

satthaina naṃ vikappaitvā, khuppipāsaṃ ahāsi mai||

5.

sauhaṃ apaitadarathau, byantībhūtau byantibhūtau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) dukhakkhamau|

assādaṃ nādhigacchāmi, phalaisvañ‍ñaisu kaisuci kaisupi (ka॰ sī॰)||

6.

sausaitvā nūna maraṇaṃ, taṃ mamaṃ āvahissati|

ambaṃ yassa phalaṃ sādu, madhuraggaṃ manauramaṃ|

yamuddhariṃ vuyhamānaṃ, udadhismā mahaṇṇavai||

7.

akkhātaṃ tai mayā sabbaṃ, yasmā upavasāmahaṃ|

rammaṃ pati nisin‍nausmi, puthulaumāyutā puthu||

8.

tvañ‍ca khau maiva mai (sī॰), mama (?) akkhāhi, attānamapalāyini|

kā vā tvamasi kalyāṇi, kissa vā tvaṃ sumajjhimai||

9.

ruppapaṭṭapalimaṭṭhīva  ruppapaṭṭap‍lamaṭṭhīva (syā॰), rūpapaṭṭapamaṭṭhīva (ka॰), byagghīva girisānujā|

yā santi nāriyau daivaisu, daivānaṃ paricārikā||

10.

yā ca manussalaukasmiṃ, rūpainānvāgatitthiyau|

rūpaina tai sadisī natthi, daivaisu gandhabbamanussalaukai daivagandhabbamānusai (syā॰)|

puṭṭhāsi mai cārupubbaṅgi, brūhi nāmañ‍ca bandhavai||

11.

yaṃ  tvaṃ pati nisin‍nausi, rammaṃ brāhmaṇa kausikiṃ|

sāhaṃ bhusālayā vutthā, varavārivahaughasā||

12.

nānādumagaṇākiṇṇā, bahukā girikandarā|

mamaiva pamukhā haunti, abhisandanti pāvusai||

13.

athau bahū vanataudā, nīlavārivahindharā|

bahukā nāgavittaudā, abhisandanti vārinā||

14.

tā ambajambulabujā, nīpā tālā cudumbarā tālamudumbarā (syā॰ ka॰)|

bahūni phalajātāni, āvahanti abhiṇhasau||

15.

yaṃ kiñ‍ci ubhatau tīrai, phalaṃ patati ambuni|

asaṃsayaṃ taṃ sautassa, phalaṃ hauti vasānugaṃ||

16.

aitadañ‍ñāya maidhāvi, puthupañ‍ña suṇauhi mai|

mā raucaya mabhisaṅgaṃ, paṭisaidha janādhipa||

17.

na vāhaṃ vaḍḍhavaṃ vaddhavaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) mañ‍ñai, yaṃ tvaṃ raṭṭhābhivaḍḍhana|

ācaiyyamānau rājisi, maraṇaṃ abhikaṅkhasi||

18.

tassa jānanti pitarau, gandhabbā ca sadaivakā|

yai  cāpi isayau laukai, sañ‍ñatattā tapassinau|

asaṃsayaṃ taipi tai (sī॰ pī॰) jānanti, paṭṭhabhūtā vaddhabhūtā (sī॰ pī॰) yasassinau||

19.

aivaṃ viditvā vidū sabbadhammaṃ, viddhaṃsanaṃ cavanaṃ jīvitassa|

na cīyatī tassa narassa pāpaṃ, sacai na caitaiti vadhāya tassa||

20.

isipūgasamañ‍ñātai, aivaṃ laukyā viditā sati pati (ka॰ syā॰ ka॰)|

anariyaparisambhāsai, pāpakammaṃ jigīsasi jigiṃsasi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

21.

sacai ahaṃ marissāmi, tīrai tai puthusussauṇi|

asaṃsayaṃ taṃ asilaukau, mayi paitai āgamissati||

22.

tasmā  hi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, rakkhassaiva rakkhassu ca (syā॰) sumajjhimai|

mā taṃ sabbau janau pacchā, pakuṭṭhāyi pakatthāsi (sī॰ pī॰), patvakkhāsi (syā॰) mayi matai||

23.

añ‍ñātamaitaṃ avisayhasāhi, attānamambañ‍ca dadāmi tai taṃ|

sau duc‍cajai kāmaguṇai pahāya, santiñ‍ca dhammañ‍ca adhiṭṭhitausi||

24.

yau hitvā pubbasañ‍ñaugaṃ, pacchā saṃyaujanai ṭhitau|

adhammañ‍caiva carati, pāpañ‍cassa pavaḍḍhati||

25.

aihi  taṃ pāpayissāmi, kāmaṃ appaussukau bhava|

upānayāmi sītasmiṃ, viharāhi anussukau||

26.

taṃ  puppharasamattaibhi, vak‍kaṅgaihi arindama|

kauñ‍cā mayūrā diviyā, kaulaṭṭhimadhusāḷikā|

kūjitā haṃsapūgaihi, kaukilaittha pabaudharai||

27.

ambaittha vippasākhaggā vippasūnaggā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), vippaaunaggā (ka॰), palālakhalasan‍nibhā|

kausambasaḷalā kausumbhasalalā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nīpā, pak‍katālavilambinau||

28.

mālī tiriṭī kāyūrī, aṅgadī candanussadau candanassadau (sī॰)|

rattiṃ tvaṃ paricāraisi, divā vaidaisi vaidanaṃ||

29.

sauḷasitthisahassāni, yā taimā paricārikā|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvausi, abbhutau laumahaṃsanau||

30.

kiṃ kammamakarī pubbai, pāpaṃ attadukhāvahaṃ|

yaṃ karitvā manussaisu, piṭṭhimaṃsāni khādasi||

31.

ajjhaināni paṭiggayha, kāmaisu gadhitau gathitau (sī॰ pī॰), giddhitau (syā॰ ka॰), giddhikau (ka॰ aṭṭha॰) ahaṃ|

acariṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ, paraisaṃ ahitāyahaṃ||

32.

yau piṭṭhimaṃsikau hauti, aivaṃ uk‍kac‍ca khādati|

yathāhaṃ aj‍ja khādāmi, piṭṭhimaṃsāni attanauti||

kiṃchandajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

512. kumbhajātakaṃ (2)

33.

kau  pāturāsī tidivā nabhamhi, aubhāsayaṃ saṃvariṃ candimāva|

gattaihi  tai rasmiyau niccharanti, satairatā satairitā (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) vij‍jurivantalikkhai||

34.

sau chin‍navātaṃ kamasī aghamhi, vaihāyasaṃ gacchasi tiṭṭhasī ca|

iddhī nu tai vatthukatā subhāvitā, anaddhagūnaṃ api daivatānaṃ||

35.

vaihāyasaṃ gammamāgamma kamamāgamma (sī॰ syā॰) tiṭṭhasi, kumbhaṃ kiṇāthāti yamaitamatthaṃ|

kau vā tuvaṃ kissa vā tāya kumbhau, akkhāhi mai brāhmaṇa aitamatthaṃ||

36.

na sappikumbhau napi tailakumbhau, na phāṇitassa na madhussa kumbhau|

kumbhassa vaj‍jāni anappakāni, dausai bahū kumbhagatai suṇātha||

37.

gaḷaiyya  yaṃ pitvā pītvā (sī॰ pī॰) patai papātaṃ, saubbhaṃ guhaṃ candaniyauḷigal‍laṃ|

bahumpi bhuñ‍jaiyya abhaujanaiyyaṃ, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

38.

yaṃ pitvā yaṃ vai pītvā (sī॰) cittasmimanaisamānau, āhiṇḍatī gauriva bhakkhasārī bhakkhasādī (sī॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰)|

anāthamānau  upagāyati nac‍cati ca, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

39.

yaṃ  vai pivitvā acailauva naggau, caraiyya gāmai visikhantarāni|

sammūḷhacittau ativailasāyī, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

40.

yaṃ pitvā uṭṭhāya pavaidhamānau, sīsañ‍ca bāhuñ‍ca bāhañ‍ca (pī॰) pacālayantau|

sau nac‍catī dārukaṭal‍lakauva, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

41.

yaṃ vai pivitvā aggidaḍḍhā sayanti, athau sigālaihipi khāditāsai|

bandhaṃ vadhaṃ bhaugajāniñ‍cupainti, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

42.

yaṃ pitvā bhāsaiyya ābhāsanaiyyaṃ, sabhāyamāsīnau apaitavatthau|

sammakkhitau samakkhitau (sī॰) vantagatau byasan‍nau, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

43.

yaṃ vai pivitvā uk‍kaṭṭhau āvilakkhau, mamaiva sabbā pathavīti mañ‍ñai mañ‍ñati (sī॰)|

na  mai samau cāturantaupi rājā, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

44.

mānātimānā kalahāni paisuṇī, dubbaṇṇinī naggayinī palāyinī|

caurāna dhuttāna gatī nikaitau, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

45.

iddhāni phītāni kulāni assu, anaikasāhassadhanāni laukai|

ucchin‍nadāyaj‍jakatānimāya, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

46.

dhañ‍ñaṃ  dhanaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ, khaittaṃ gavaṃ yattha vināsayanti|

ucchaidanī vittavataṃ ucchaidanī vittagataṃ (syā॰), ucchaidanivittagataṃ (ka॰) kulānaṃ, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

47.

yaṃ vai pitvā dittarūpauva duṭṭharūpauva (sī॰) pausau, ak‍kausati mātaraṃ pitarañ‍ca|

sassumpi gaṇhaiyya athaupi suṇhaṃ, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

48.

yaṃ vai pitvā dittarūpāva nārī, ak‍kausati sasuraṃ sāmikañ‍ca|

dāsampi  gaṇhai paricārakampi, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

49.

yaṃ vai pivitvāna yañ‍cai pītvāna (pī॰) hanaiyya pausau, dhammai ṭhitaṃ samaṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vā|

gacchai apāyampi tataunidānaṃ, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

50.

yaṃ vai pivitvā duc‍caritaṃ caranti, kāyaina vācāya ca caitasā ca|

nirayaṃ vajanti duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

51.

yaṃ  yācamānā na labhanti pubbai, bahuṃ hirañ‍ñampi paric‍cajantā|

sau taṃ pivitvā alikaṃ bhaṇāti, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

52.

yaṃ vai pitvā paisanai paisiyantau, ac‍cāyikai karaṇīyamhi jātai|

atthampi sau nappajānāti vuttau, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

53.

hirīmanāpi  ahirīkabhāvaṃ, pātuṃ karaunti madanāya madirāya (pī॰) mattā|

dhīrāpi  santā bahukaṃ bhaṇanti, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

54.

yaṃ vai pitvā aikathūpā sayanti, anāsakā thaṇḍiladukkhasaiyyaṃ|

dubbaṇṇiyaṃ āyasakyañ‍cupainti, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

55.

yaṃ vai pitvā pattakhandhā sayanti, gāvau kuṭahatāva na hi vāruṇiyā|

yaṃ vai pitvā pattakkhandhā, sayanti gāvau kūṭahatāriva| na hi vāruṇiyā vaigau, naraina sussahauriva| (sī॰) vaigau naraina susahauriva yaṃ vai pitvā pattakkhandhā, sayanti gāvau kūṭahatāriva| na hi vāruṇiyā vaigau, naraina sussahauriva| (sī॰), tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

56.

yaṃ manussā vivaj‍jainti, sappaṃ ghauravisaṃ miva visamiva (syā॰), visaṃ iva (ka॰), visāmiva (?)|

taṃ laukai visasamānaṃ, kau narau pātumarahati||

57.

yaṃ vai pitvā andhakavaiṇḍaputtā, samuddatīrai paricārayantā andhakavaiṇhuputtā (sī॰ pī॰), aṇḍakavaiṇḍaputtā (ka॰)|

upak‍kamuṃ musalaihañ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ, tassā puṇṇaṃ kumbhamimaṃ kiṇātha||

58.

yaṃ vai pitvā pubbadaivā pamattā, tidivā cutā sassatiyā samāya|

taṃ  tādisaṃ maj‍jamimaṃ niratthakaṃ, jānaṃ mahārāja kathaṃ pivaiyya||

59.

nayimasmiṃ kumbhasmiṃ dadhi vā madhu vā, aivaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kiṇāhi rāja|

aivañhimaṃ kumbhagatā mayā tai, akkhātarūpaṃ tava sabbamitta||

60.

na  mai pitā vā athavāpi mātā, aitādisā yādisakau tuvaṃsi|

hitānukampī paramatthakāmau, sauhaṃ karissaṃ vacanaṃ tavaj‍ja||

61.

dadāmi tai gāmavarāni pañ‍ca, dāsīsataṃ satta gavaṃ satāni|

ājañ‍ñayuttai ca rathai dasa imai, ācariyau hausi mamatthakāmau||

62.

tavaiva dāsīsatamatthu rāja, gāmā ca gāvau ca tavaiva hauntu|

ājañ‍ñayuttā ca rathā tavaiva, sak‍kauhamasmī tidasānamindau||

63.

maṃsaudanaṃ sappipāyāsaṃ sappipāyañ‍ca (sī॰) bhuñ‍ja, khādassu ca tvaṃ madhumāsapūvai|

aivaṃ  tuvaṃ dhammaratau janinda, aninditau saggamupaihi ṭhānanti||

kumbhajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ|

513. jayaddisajātakaṃ (3)

64.

cirassaṃ  vata mai udapādi aj‍ja, bhakkhau mahā sattamibhattakālai|

kutausi kau vāsi tadiṅgha brūhi, ācikkha jātiṃ viditau yathāsi||

65.

pañ‍cālarājā migavaṃ paviṭṭhau, jayaddisau nāma yadissutau tai|

carāmi kacchāni vanāni cāhaṃ, pasadaṃ imaṃ khāda mamaj‍ja muñ‍ca||

66.

sainaiva  tvaṃ paṇasi sassamānau sayhamānau (syā॰ ka॰), mamaisa bhakkhau pasadau yaṃ vadaisi|

taṃ khādiyāna pasadaṃ jighañ‍ñaṃ jighacchaṃ (?), khādissaṃ pacchā na vilāpakālau||

67.

na catthi maukkhau mama nik‍kayaina vik‍kayaina (sī॰), gantvāna pac‍cāgamanāya paṇhai|

taṃ  saṅkaraṃ saṅgaraṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇassappadāya, sac‍cānurakkhī punarāvajissaṃ||

68.

kiṃ kammajātaṃ anutappatai tvaṃ anutappatī taṃ (sī॰ pī॰), pattaṃ samīpaṃ maraṇassa rāja|

ācikkha mai taṃ api sak‍kuṇaimu, anujānituṃ āgamanāya paṇhai||

69.

katā mayā brāhmaṇassa dhanāsā, taṃ saṅkaraṃ paṭimuk‍kaṃ na muttaṃ|

taṃ saṅkaraṃ brāhmaṇassappadāya, sac‍cānurakkhī punarāvajissaṃ||

70.

yā tai katā brāhmaṇassa dhanāsā, taṃ saṅkaraṃ paṭimuk‍kaṃ na muttaṃ|

taṃ saṅkaraṃ brāhmaṇassappadāya, sac‍cānurakkhī punarāvajassu||

71.

muttau ca sau paurisādassa purisādassa (pī॰) hatthā, gantvā sakaṃ mandiraṃ kāmakāmī|

taṃ saṅkaraṃ brāhmaṇassappadāya, āmantayī puttamalīnasattaṃ sattuṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)||

72.

aj‍jaiva  raj‍jaṃ abhisiñ‍cayassu, dhammaṃ cara saisu paraisu cāpi|

adhammakārau  ca tai māhu raṭṭhai, gacchāmahaṃ paurisādassa ñattai ñantai (syā॰)||

73.

kiṃ kamma kubbaṃ tava daiva pāva daivapādai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), nārādhayī taṃ tadicchāmi sautuṃ|

yamaj‍ja raj‍jamhi udassayai tuvaṃ, raj‍jampi nicchaiyyaṃ tayā vināhaṃ||

74.

na kammunā vā vacasā va tāta, aparādhitauhaṃ tuviyaṃ sarāmi|

sandhiñ‍ca saddhiṃ ca (ka॰) katvā purisādakaina, sac‍cānurakkhī punāhaṃ gamissaṃ||

75.

ahaṃ gamissāmi idhaiva hauhi, natthi tatau jīvatau vippamaukkhau|

sacai tuvaṃ gacchasiyaiva rāja, ahampi gacchāmi ubhau na hauma||

76.

addhā hi tāta satānaisa dhammau, maraṇā ca mai dukkhataraṃ tadassa|

kammāsapādau taṃ yadā pacitvā, pasayha khādai bhidā rukkhasūlai||

77.

pāṇaina tai pāṇamahaṃ nimissaṃ, mā tvaṃ agā paurisādassa ñattai|

aitañ‍ca  tai pāṇamahaṃ nimissaṃ, tasmā mataṃ jīvitassa vaṇṇaimi varaimi (sī॰)||

78.

tatau  havai dhitimā rājaputtau, vanditvā mātu ca pitu ca vandittha mātuc‍ca pituc‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) pādai|

dukhinissa mātā nipatā nipatī (sī॰ pī॰) pathabyā, pitāssa paggayha bhujāni kandati||

79.

taṃ  gacchantaṃ tāva pitā viditvā, parammukhau vandati pañ‍jalīkau|

saumau ca rājā varuṇau ca rājā, pajāpatī candimā sūriyau ca|

aitaihi guttau purisādakamhā, anuñ‍ñātau sautthi pac‍caihi tāta||

80.

yaṃ daṇḍakirañ‍ñau gatassa yaṃ daṇḍakārañ‍ñagatassa (pī॰) mātā, rāmassakāsi sautthānaṃ suguttā|

taṃ tai ahaṃ sautthānaṃ karaumi, aitaina sac‍caina sarantu daivā|

anuñ‍ñātau sautthi pac‍caihi putta||

81.

āvī rahau vāpī manaupadausaṃ, nāhaṃ sarai jātu malīnasattai|

aitaina sac‍caina sarantu daivā, anuñ‍ñātau sautthi pac‍caihi bhātika bhāta (sī॰), bhātā (syā॰ pī॰)||

82.

yasmā ca mai anadhimanausi sāmi, na cāpi mai manasā appiyausi|

aitaina sac‍caina sarantu daivā, anuñ‍ñātau  sautthi pac‍caihi sāmi||

83.

brahā ujū cārumukhau kutausi, na maṃ pajānāsi vanai vasantaṃ|

luddaṃ maṃ ñatvā ‘‘purisādakau’’ti, kau sautthi mājānamidhā’vajaiyya||

84.

jānāmi ludda purisādakau tvaṃ, na taṃ na jānāmi vanai vasantaṃ|

ahañ‍ca puttausmi jayaddisassa, mamaj‍ja khāda pitunau pamaukkhā||

85.

jānāmi  puttauti puttausi (syā॰ ka॰) jayaddisassa, tathā hi vau mukhavaṇṇau ubhin‍naṃ|

suduk‍karañ‍ñaiva suduk‍karañ‍caiva (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) kataṃ tavaidaṃ, yau mattumicchai pitunau pamaukkhā||

86.

na duk‍karaṃ kiñ‍ci mahaittha mañ‍ñai, yau mattumicchai pitunau pamaukkhā|

mātu ca mātuc‍ca (sī॰) haitu paralauka gantvā gamyā (sī॰ syā॰), gamya (pī॰), sukhaina  saggaina ca sampayuttau||

87.

ahañ‍ca khau attanau pāpakiriyaṃ, āvī rahau vāpi sarai na jātu|

saṅkhātajātīmaraṇauhamasmi, yathaiva mai idha tathā parattha||

88.

khādaj‍ja maṃ dāni mahānubhāva, karassu kic‍cāni imaṃ sarīraṃ|

rukkhassa vā tai papatāmi aggā, chādayamānau mayhaṃ tvamadaisi maṃsaṃ||

89.

idañ‍ca tai ruc‍cati rājaputta, cajaisi cajāsi (sī॰ pī॰) pāṇaṃ pitunau pamaukkhā|

tasmā hi sau tasmātiha (sī॰ syā॰) tvaṃ taramānarūpau, sambhañ‍ja kaṭṭhāni jalaihi aggiṃ||

90.

tatau havai dhitimā rājaputtau, dāruṃ samāhatvā mahantamaggiṃ|

sandīpayitvā paṭivaidayittha, ādīpitau dāni mahāyamaggi mayā yakkhamaggi (ka॰)||

91.

khādaj‍ja  maṃ dāni pasayhakārī, kiṃ maṃ muhuṃ paikkhasi haṭṭhalaumau|

tathā tathā tuyhamahaṃ karaumi, yathā  yathā maṃ chādayamānau adaisi||

92.

kau tādisaṃ arahati khāditāyai, dhammai ṭhitaṃ sac‍cavādiṃ vadañ‍ñuṃ|

muddhāpi tassa viphalaiyya sattadhā, yau tādisaṃ sac‍cavādiṃ adaiyya||

93.

idañhi sau brāhmaṇaṃ mañ‍ñamānau, sasau avāsaisi sakai sarīrai|

tainaiva sau candimā daivaputtau, sasatthutau sasaṭṭhakau (syā॰) kāmaduhaj‍ja kāmaruhaj‍ja (ka॰) yakkha||

94.

candau yathā rāhumukhā pamuttau, viraucatai pan‍narasaiva bhāṇumā bhānumā (sī॰ pī॰)|

aivaṃ tuvaṃ paurisādā pamuttau, virauca kapilai kampil‍la (sī॰ pī॰), kapil‍lai (syā॰) mahānubhāva|

āmaudayaṃ pitaraṃ mātarañ‍ca, sabbau ca tai nandatu ñātipakkhau||

95.

tatau havai dhitimā rājaputtau, katañ‍jalī pariyāya pariyagā (sī॰), paggayha (syā॰ pī॰) paurisādaṃ|

anuñ‍ñātau sautthi sukhī araugau, pac‍cāgamā pac‍cāga (pī॰) kapilamalīnasattā kampil‍la’malīnasattau (sī॰ pī॰), kapila’malīnasattā (ka॰)||

96.

taṃ  naigamā jānapadā ca sabbai, hatthārauhā rathikā pattikā ca|

namassamānā pañ‍jalikā upāgamuṃ, namatthu tai duk‍karakārakausīti||

jayaddisajātakaṃ jayadisajātakaṃ (ka॰) tatiyaṃ|

514. chaddantajātakaṃ (4)

97.

kiṃ  nu saucasinuc‍caṅgi, paṇḍūsi varavaṇṇini|

milāyasi visālakkhi, mālāva parimadditā||

98.

dauhaḷau mai mahārāja, supinantainupajjhagā nu’pac‍cagā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

na sau sulabharūpauva, yādisau mama dauhaḷau||

99.

yai kaici mānusā kāmā, idha laukasmi nandanai|

sabbai tai pacurā mayhaṃ, ahaṃ tai dammi dauhaḷaṃ||

100.

luddā daiva samāyantu, yai kaici vijitai tava|

aitaisaṃ ahamakkhissaṃ, yādisau mama dauhaḷau||

101.

imai tai luddakā daivi, katahatthā visāradā|

vanañ‍ñū ca migañ‍ñū ca, mamatthai mama tai (pī॰) cattajīvitā||

102.

luddaputtā nisāmaitha, yāvantaittha samāgatā|

chabbisāṇaṃ gajaṃ saitaṃ, addasaṃ supinai ahaṃ|

tassa dantaihi mai atthau, alābhai natthi jīvitaṃ||

103.

na  nau pitūnaṃ na pitāmahānaṃ, diṭṭhau sutau kuñ‍jarau chabbisāṇau|

yamaddasā supinai rājaputtī, akkhāhi nau yādisau hatthināgau||

104.

disā catassau vidisā catassau, uddhaṃ adhau dasa disā imāyau|

katamaṃ disaṃ tiṭṭhati nāgarājā, yamaddasā supinai chabbisāṇaṃ||

105.

itau ujuṃ uttariyaṃ disāyaṃ, atik‍kamma sau sattagirī brahantai|

suvaṇṇapassau nāma girī uḷārau, supupphitau sampupphitau (syā॰) kimpurisānuciṇṇau||

106.

āruyha  sailaṃ bhavanaṃ kin‍narānaṃ, aulaukaya pabbatapādamūlaṃ|

atha dakkhasī maighasamānavaṇṇaṃ, nigraudharājaṃ atha sahassapādaṃ pauraṃ (ka॰)||

107.

tatthacchatī kuñ‍jarau chabbisāṇau, sabbasaitau duppasahau paraibhi|

rakkhanti naṃ aṭṭhasahassanāgā, īsādantā vātajavappahārinau||

108.

tiṭṭhanti  tai tumūlaṃ tumūla (syā॰), bhimūla (pī॰ ka॰) passasantā, kuppanti vātassapi airitassa|

manussabhūtaṃ pana tattha disvā, bhasmaṃ karaiyyuṃ nāssa rajaupi tassa||

109.

bahū himai rājakulamhi santi, piḷandhanā jātarūpassa daivī|

muttāmaṇīvaiḷuriyāmayā ca, kiṃ kāhasi dantapiḷandhanaina|

māraitukāmā kuñ‍jaraṃ chabbisāṇaṃ, udāhu ghātaissasi luddaputtai||

110.

sā issitā dukkhitā casmi ludda, uddhañ‍ca sussāmi anussarantī|

karauhi mai luddaka aitamatthaṃ, dassāmi tai gāmavarāni pañ‍ca||

111.

katthacchatī katthamupaiti ṭhānaṃ, vīthissa kā nhāna nahāna (sī॰ pī॰) gatassa hauti|

kathañhi sau nhāyati nahāyati (sī॰ pī॰) nāgarājā, kathaṃ vijānaimu gatiṃ gajassa||

112.

tatthaiva  sā paukkharaṇī adūrai avidūrai (syā॰ ka॰), rammā sutitthā ca mahaudikā mahaudakā (syā॰ ka॰) ca|

sampupphitā  bhamaragaṇānuciṇṇā kiṇṇā (katthaci), aittha hi sau nhāyati nāgarājā||

113.

sīsaṃ nahātuppala nahātau uppala (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) mālabhārī, sabbasaitau puṇḍarīkattacaṅgī|

āmaudamānau gacchati san‍nikaitaṃ, purakkhatvā mahaisiṃ sabbabhaddaṃ||

114.

tatthaiva sau uggahaitvāna vākyaṃ, ādāya tūṇiñ‍ca dhanuñ‍ca luddau|

vituriyati vituriya sau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) sattagirī brahantai, suvaṇṇapassaṃ nāma giriṃ uḷāraṃ||

115.

āruyha sailaṃ bhavanaṃ kin‍narānaṃ, aulaukayī pabbatapādamūlaṃ|

tatthaddasā maighasamānavaṇṇaṃ, nigraudharājaṃ aṭṭhasahassapādaṃ||

116.

tatthaddasā kuñ‍jaraṃ chabbisāṇaṃ, sabbasaitaṃ duppasahaṃ paraibhi|

rakkhanti naṃ aṭṭhasahassanāgā, īsādantā vātajavappahārinau||

117.

tatthaddasā  paukkharaṇiṃ adūrai, rammaṃ sutitthañ‍ca mahaudikañ‍ca|

sampupphitaṃ  bhamaragaṇānuciṇṇaṃ, yattha hi sau nhāyati nāgarājā||

118.

disvāna nāgassa gatiṃ ṭhitiñ‍ca, vīthissa yā nhānagatassa hauti|

aupātamāgacchi anariyarūpau, payaujitau cittavasānugāya||

119.

khaṇitvāna  kāsuṃ phalakaihi chādayi, attānamaudhāya dhanuñ‍ca luddau|

passāgataṃ puthusal‍laina nāgaṃ, samappayī duk‍kaṭakammakārī||

120.

viddhau ca nāgau kauñ‍camanādi ghauraṃ, sabbai ca sabbaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nāgā nin‍naduṃ nin‍nādu (syā॰), nin‍nādaṃ (ka॰) ghaurarūpaṃ|

tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca raṇaṃ cuṇṇaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) karauntā, dhāviṃsu tai aṭṭhadisā samantatau||

121.

vadhissamaitanti vadhissamainanti (syā॰) parāmasantau, kāsāvamaddakkhi dhajaṃ isīnaṃ|

dukkhaina phuṭṭhassudapādi sañ‍ñā, arahaddhajau sabbhi avajjharūpau||

122.

anik‍kasāvau kāsāvaṃ, yau vatthaṃ paridahissati paridahaissati (sī॰ pī॰), paridhassati (katthaci)|

apaitau damasac‍caina, na sau kāsāvamarahati||

123.

yau  ca vantakasāvassa, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

upaitau damasac‍caina, sa vai kāsāvamarahati||

124.

samappitau puthusal‍laina nāgau, aduṭṭhacittau luddakamajjhabhāsi|

kimatthayaṃ kissa vā samma haitu, mamaṃ vadhī kassa vāyaṃ payaugau||

125.

kāsissa rañ‍ñau mahaisī bhadantai, sā pūjitā rājakulai subhaddā|

taṃ addasā sā ca mamaṃ asaṃsi, dantaihi atthauti ca maṃ atthauti mamaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) avauca||

126.

bahū  hi mai dantayugā uḷārā, yai mai pitūnañ‍ca pitun‍nampi (pī॰) pitāmahānaṃ|

jānāti sā kaudhanā rājaputtī, vadhatthikā vairamakāsi bālā||

127.

uṭṭhaihi tvaṃ ludda kharaṃ gahaitvā, dantai imai chinda purā marāmi|

vaj‍jāsi taṃ kaudhanaṃ rājaputtiṃ, ‘‘nāgau hatau handa imassa dantā’’||

128.

uṭṭhāya sau luddau kharaṃ gahaitvā, chaitvāna  dantāni gajuttamassa|

vaggū subhai appaṭimai pathabyā, ādāya pak‍kāmi tatau hi khippaṃ||

129.

bhayaṭṭitā bhayadditā (sī॰ pī॰) nāgavadhaina aṭṭā, yai tai nāgā aṭṭha disā vidhāvuṃ|

adisvāna adisva (sī॰ pī॰) pausaṃ gajapac‍camittaṃ, pac‍cāgamuṃ yaina sau nāgarājā||

130.

tai tattha kanditvā rauditvāna rauditva (sī॰ pī॰) nāgā, sīsai sakai paṃsukaṃ aukiritvā|

agamaṃsu tai sabbai sakaṃ nikaitaṃ, purakkhatvā mahaisiṃ sabbabhaddaṃ||

131.

ādāya  dantāni gajuttamassa, vaggū subhai appaṭimai pathabyā|

suvaṇṇarājīhi samantamaudarai, sau luddakau kāsipuraṃ upāgami|

upanaisi sau rājakañ‍ñāya dantai, nāgau hatau handa imassa dantā||

132.

disvāna  dantāni gajuttamassa, bhattuppiyassa purimāya jātiyā|

tatthaiva tassā hadayaṃ aphāli, tainaiva  sā kālamakāsi bālā||

133.

sambaudhipattau sa ca (sī॰ syā॰), va (pī॰) mahānubhāvau, sitaṃ akāsi parisāya majjhai|

pucchiṃsu bhikkhū suvimuttacittā, nākāraṇai pātukaraunti buddhā||

134.

yamaddasātha dahariṃ kumāriṃ, kāsāyavatthaṃ anagāriyaṃ carantiṃ|

sā khau tadā rājakañ‍ñā ahausi, ahaṃ tadā nāgarājā ahausiṃ||

135.

ādāya dantāni gajuttamassa, vaggū subhai appaṭimai pathabyā|

yau luddakau kāsipuraṃ upāgami, sau khau tadā daivadattau ahausi||

136.

anāvasūraṃ cirarattasaṃsitaṃ, uc‍cāvacaṃ ucaṃ nīcaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) caritamidaṃ purāṇaṃ|

vītaddarau vītasaukau visal‍lau, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya abhāsi buddhau||

137.

ahaṃ vau taina kālaina, ahausiṃ tattha bhikkhavau|

nāgarājā tadā haumi hausiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), aivaṃ dhāraitha jātakanti||

chaddantajātakaṃ catutthaṃ|

515. sambhavajātakaṃ (5)

138.

raj‍jañ‍ca  paṭipan‍nāsma, ādhipac‍caṃ sucīrata|

mahattaṃ pattumicchāmi, vijaituṃ pathaviṃ imaṃ||

139.

dhammaina nau adhammaina, adhammau mai na ruc‍cati|

kic‍cauva dhammau caritau, rañ‍ñau hauti sucīrata||

140.

idha  caivāninditā yaina, paic‍ca yaina aninditā|

yasaṃ daivamanussaisu, yaina pappaumu pappaimu (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) brāhmaṇa||

141.

yauhaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, kattumicchāmi brāhmaṇa|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, brāhmaṇakkhāhi pucchitau||

142.

nāñ‍ñatra vidhurā rāja, aitadakkhātumarahati|

yaṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, kattumicchasi khattiya||

143.

aihi  khau pahitau gaccha, vidhurassa upantikaṃ|

nikkhañ‍cimaṃ nikkhaṃ ratta (sī॰), nikkhamimaṃ (pī॰) suvaṇṇassa, haraṃ gaccha sucīrata|

abhihāraṃ imaṃ daj‍jā, atthadhammānusiṭṭhiyā||

144.

svādhippāgā bhāradvājau, vidhurassa upantikaṃ|

tamaddasa mahābrahmā, asamānaṃ sakai gharai||

145.

rañ‍ñauhaṃ pahitau dūtau, kaurabyassa yasassinau|

‘‘atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchaisi’’, ic‍cabravi yudhiṭṭhilau|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, vidhurakkhāhi pucchitau||

146.

gaṅgaṃ mai pidahissanti, na taṃ sak‍kaumi brāhmaṇa|

apidhaituṃ  mahāsindhuṃ, taṃ kathaṃ sau bhavissati||

147.

na tai sak‍kaumi akkhātuṃ, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchitau|

bhadrakārau ca mai puttau, aurasau mama atrajau|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, gantvā pucchassu brāhmaṇa||

148.

svādhippāgā bhāradvājau, bhadrakārassupantikaṃ bhadrakārassa santikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

tamaddasa mahābrahmā, nisin‍naṃ samhi vaismani||

149.

rañ‍ñauhaṃ pahitau dūtau, kaurabyassa yasassinau|

‘‘atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchaisi’’, ic‍cabravi yudhiṭṭhilau|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, bhadrakāra pabrūhi bravīhi (sī॰ pī॰) mai||

150.

maṃsakājaṃ maṃsakācaṃ (pī॰) avahāya, gaudhaṃ anupatāmahaṃ|

na tai sak‍kaumi akkhātuṃ, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchitau||

151.

sañ‍cayau  sañ‍jayau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nāma mai bhātā, kaniṭṭhau mai sucīrata|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, gantvā pucchassu brāhmaṇa||

152.

svādhippāgā bhāradvājau, sañ‍cayassa upantikaṃ|

tamaddasa mahābrahmā, nisin‍naṃ samhi vaismani parisati (syā॰), parīsati (pī॰)||

153.

rañ‍ñauhaṃ pahitau dūtau, kaurabyassa yasassinau|

‘‘atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchaisi’’, ic‍cabravi yudhiṭṭhilau|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, sañ‍cayakkhāhi pucchitau||

154.

sadā maṃ gilatai gilatī (sī॰), gilati (pī॰) mac‍cu, sāyaṃ pātau sucīrata|

na tai sak‍kaumi akkhātuṃ, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchitau||

155.

sambhavau  nāma mai bhātā, kaniṭṭhau mai sucīrata|

taṃ tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, gantvā pucchassu brāhmaṇa||

156.

abbhutau vata bhau dhammau, nāyaṃ asmāka ruc‍cati|

tayau janā pitāputtā, taisu pañ‍ñāya nau vidū||

157.

na taṃ sak‍kautha akkhātuṃ, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchitā|

kathaṃ nu daharau jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchitau||

158.

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi mañ‍ñāsi (syā॰ ka॰), apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

159.

yathāpi candau vimalau, gacchaṃ ākāsadhātuyā|

sabbai tārāgaṇai laukai, ābhāya atiraucati||

160.

aivampi daharūpaitau, pañ‍ñāyaugaina sambhavau|

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi, apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

161.

yathāpi rammakau māsau, gimhānaṃ hauti brāhmaṇa|

ataivañ‍ñaihi māsaihi, dumapupphaihi saubhati||

162.

aivampi  daharūpaitau, pañ‍ñāyaugaina sambhavau|

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi, apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

163.

yathāpi himavā brahmai, pabbatau gandhamādanau|

nānārukkhaihi sañchan‍nau, mahābhūtagaṇālayau|

ausadhaihi  ca dibbaihi, disā bhāti pavāti ca||

164.

aivampi daharūpaitau, pañ‍ñāyaugaina sambhavau|

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi, apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

165.

yathāpi pāvakau brahmai, ac‍cimālī yasassimā|

jalamānau vanai gacchai caraṃ kacchai (pī॰), analau kaṇhavattanī||

166.

ghatāsanau dhūmakaitu, uttamāhaivanandahau|

nisīthai nissīvai (syā॰), nisivai (ka॰) pabbataggasmiṃ, pahūtaidhau bahutaijau (syā॰ ka॰) viraucati||

167.

aivampi  daharūpaitau, pañ‍ñāyaugaina sambhavau|

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi, apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

168.

javaina bhadraṃ jānanti, balibaddañ‍ca balivaddañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) vāhiyai|

dauhaina dhainuṃ jānanti, bhāsamānañ‍ca paṇḍitaṃ||

169.

aivampi daharūpaitau, pañ‍ñāyaugaina sambhavau|

mā naṃ daharauti uñ‍ñāsi, apucchitvāna sambhavaṃ|

pucchitvā sambhavaṃ jañ‍ñā, atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca brāhmaṇa||

170.

svādhippāgā bhāradvājau, sambhavassa upantikaṃ|

tamaddasa mahābrahmā, kīḷamānaṃ bahīpurai||

171.

rañ‍ñauhaṃ pahitau dūtau, kaurabyassa yasassinau|

‘‘atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca pucchaisi’’, ic‍cabravi yudhiṭṭhilau|

taṃ  tvaṃ atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, sambhavakkhāhi pucchitau||

172.

taggha  tai ahamakkhissaṃ, yathāpi kusalau tathā|

rājā ca khau taṃ jānāti, yadi kāhati vā na vā||

173.

‘‘aj‍ja suvai’’ti saṃsaiyya, rañ‍ñā puṭṭhau sucīrata|

mā katvā avasī rājā, atthai jātai yudhiṭṭhilau||

174.

ajjhattañ‍ñaiva saṃsaiyya, rañ‍ñā puṭṭhau sucīrata|

kummaggaṃ na nivaisaiyya, yathā mūḷhau acaitasau acaitanau (ka॰)||

175.

attānaṃ nātivattaiyya, adhammaṃ na samācarai|

atitthai nappatāraiyya, anatthai na yutau siyā||

176.

yau ca aitāni ṭhānāni, kattuṃ jānāti khattiyau|

sadā sau vaḍḍhatai rājā, suk‍kapakkhaiva candimā||

177.

ñātīnañ‍ca piyau hauti, mittaisu ca viraucati|

kāyassa bhaidā sappañ‍ñau, saggaṃ sau upapaj‍jatīti||

sambhavajātakaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

516. mahākapijātakaṃ (6)

178.

bārāṇasyaṃ bārāṇassaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ahū rājā, kāsīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau|

mittāmac‍caparibyūḷhau, agamāsi migājinaṃ migājiraṃ (sī॰), migāciraṃ (pī॰)||

179.

tattha  brāhmaṇamaddakkhi, saitaṃ citraṃ kilāsinaṃ|

viddhastaṃ kauviḷāraṃva, kisaṃ dhamanisanthataṃ||

180.

paramakāruñ‍ñataṃ  pattaṃ, disvā kicchagataṃ naraṃ|

avaca byamhitau rājā, ‘‘yakkhānaṃ katamau nusi||

181.

‘‘hatthapādā ca tai saitā, tatau saitataraṃ saitatarau (pī॰) sirau|

gattaṃ kammāsavaṇṇaṃ tai, kilāsabahulau casi||

182.

‘‘vaṭṭanāvaḷi vaṭṭhanāvali (pī॰) saṅkāsā, piṭṭhi tai nin‍natun‍natā|

kāḷapabbāva kāḷapabbā ca (syā॰), kāḷā pabbā ca (pī॰) tai aṅgā, nāñ‍ñaṃ passāmi aidisaṃ||

183.

‘‘ugghaṭṭapādau  tasitau, kisau dhamanisanthatau|

chātau ātattarūpausi ādittarūpausi (ka॰), atittarūpausi (syā॰ ka॰ aṭṭha॰), kutausi kattha gacchasi||

184.

‘‘duddasī appakārausi, dubbaṇṇau bhīmadassanau|

janaitti yāpi tai mātā, na taṃ icchaiyya passituṃ||

185.

‘‘kiṃ kammamakaraṃ kammamakarā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pubbai, kaṃ avajjhaṃ aghātayi|

kibbisaṃ yaṃ karitvāna, idaṃ dukkhaṃ upāgami’’||

186.

taggha tai ahamakkhissaṃ, yathāpi kusalau tathā|

sac‍cavādiñhi laukasmiṃ, pasaṃsantīdha paṇḍitā||

187.

aikau caraṃ gaugavaisau, mūḷhau ac‍casariṃ vanai|

arañ‍ñai irīṇai īriṇai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vivanai, nānākuñ‍jarasaivitai||

188.

vāḷamigānucaritai, vippanaṭṭhausmi kānanai|

acariṃ tattha sattāhaṃ, khuppipāsa khuppipāsā (sī॰ pī॰) samappitau||

189.

tattha tindukamaddakkhiṃ, visamaṭṭhaṃ bubhukkhitau|

papātamabhilambantaṃ, sampan‍naphaladhārinaṃ||

190.

vātassitāni  bhakkhaisiṃ, tāni ruc‍ciṃsu mai bhusaṃ|

atittau rukkhamārūhiṃ māruyha (sī॰ syā॰), tattha haissāmi āsitau||

191.

aikaṃ mai bhakkhitaṃ āsi, dutiyaṃ abhipatthitaṃ|

tatau sā bhañ‍jatha sākhā, chin‍nā pharasunā viya||

192.

sauhaṃ sahāva sākhāhi, uddhaṃpādau avaṃsirau|

appatiṭṭhai anālambai, giriduggasmi pāpataṃ||

193.

yasmā ca vāri gambhīraṃ, tasmā na samapaj‍jisaṃ samapaj‍jasiṃ (sī॰), samabhaj‍jisaṃ (pī॰)|

tattha saisiṃ nirānandau, anūnā anāthau (sī॰) dasa rattiyau||

194.

athaittha  kapi māgañchi māgacchi (syā॰ ka॰), gaunaṅgulau darīcarau|

sākhāhi sākhaṃ vicarantau, khādamānau dumapphalaṃ||

195.

sau  maṃ disvā kisaṃ paṇḍuṃ, kāruñ‍ñamakaraṃ mayi|

ambhau kau nāma sau aittha, aivaṃ dukkhaina aṭṭitau||

196.

manussau amanussau vā, attānaṃ mai pavaidaya|

tassañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

197.

manussauhaṃ byasampattau vasampattau (syā॰ ka॰), sā mai natthi itau gati|

taṃ vau vadāmi bhaddaṃ vau, tvañ‍ca mai saraṇaṃ bhava||

198.

garuṃ garu (sī॰ pī॰) silaṃ gahaitvāna, vicarī vicari (pī॰) pabbatai kapi|

silāya yauggaṃ katvāna, nisabhau aitadabravi||

199.

aihi mai piṭṭhimāruyha, gīvaṃ gaṇhāhi bāhubhi|

ahaṃ taṃ uddharissāmi, giriduggata vaigasā||

200.

tassa  taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vānarindassa sirīmatau|

piṭṭhimāruyha dhīrassa, gīvaṃ bāhāhi aggahiṃ||

201.

sau maṃ tatau samuṭṭhāsi, taijassī taijasī (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) balavā kapi|

vihañ‍ñamānau kicchaina, giriduggata vaigasā||

202.

uddharitvāna maṃ santau, nisabhau aitadabravi|

iṅgha maṃ samma rakkhassu, passupissaṃ muhuttakaṃ||

203.

sīhā byagghā ca dīpī ca, acchakaukataracchayau|

tai maṃ pamattaṃ hiṃsaiyyuṃ, tai tvaṃ disvā nivāraya disvāna vāraya (pī॰)||

204.

aivaṃ mai parittātūna parittātuna (ka॰), passupi sau muhuttakaṃ|

tadāhaṃ pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ, paṭilacchiṃ ayaunisau||

205.

bhakkhau ayaṃ manussānaṃ, yathā cañ‍ñai vanai migā|

yaṃ nūnimaṃ vadhitvāna, chātau khādaiyya vānaraṃ||

206.

asitau āsikau (pī॰) ca gamissāmi, maṃsamādāya sambalaṃ|

kantāraṃ nittharissāmi, pāthaiyyaṃ mai bhavissati||

207.

tatau silaṃ gahaitvāna, matthakaṃ san‍nitāḷayiṃ|

mama gatta bhatta (sī॰ syā॰), hattha (pī॰) kilantassa, pahārau dubbalau ahu||

208.

sau  ca vaigainudappattau, kapi ruhira rudhira (sī॰) makkhitau|

assupuṇṇaihi naittaihi, raudantau maṃ udikkhati||

209.

māyyaumaṃ kari bhaddantai, tvañ‍ca nāmaidisaṃ kari|

tvañ‍ca khau nāma dīghāvu dīghāyu (pī॰), añ‍ñai añ‍ñaṃ (pī॰) vāraitumarahasi||

210.

ahau  vata rai purisa, tāva duk‍karakāraka|

aidisā visamā duggā, papātā uddhatau uddhaṭau (pī॰) mayā||

211.

ānītau paralaukāva, dubbhaiyyaṃ maṃ amañ‍ñatha|

taṃ taina pāpadhammaina, pāpaṃ pāpaina cintitaṃ||

212.

mā haiva tvaṃ adhammaṭṭha, vaidanaṃ kaṭukaṃ phusi|

mā haiva pāpakammaṃ taṃ, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃva taṃ vadhi||

213.

tayimai natthi vissāsau, pāpadhamma asañ‍ñata pāpadhammaṃ amañ‍ñatha (pī॰)|

aihi mai piṭṭhitau gaccha, dissamānauva santikai||

214.

muttausi hatthā vāḷānaṃ, pattausi mānusiṃ padaṃ|

aisa maggau adhammaṭṭha, taina gaccha yathāsukhaṃ||

215.

idaṃ vatvā giricarau, rahadai ruhiraṃ (syā॰ ka॰) pakkhalya matthakaṃ|

assūni sampamaj‍jitvā, tatau pabbatamāruhi||

216.

sauhaṃ tainābhisattausmi, pariḷāhaina aṭṭitau|

ḍayhamānaina gattaina, vāriṃ pātuṃ upāgamiṃ||

217.

agginā viya santattau, rahadau ruhiramakkhitau|

pubbalauhitasaṅkāsau, sabbau mai samapaj‍jatha||

218.

yāvantau udabindūni, kāyasmiṃ nipatiṃsu mai|

tāvantau gaṇḍa gaṇḍū (sī॰ pī॰), gaṇḍu (syā॰) jāyaitha, addhabailuvasādisā||

219.

pabhin‍nā pagghariṃsu mai, kuṇapā pubbalauhitā|

yaina yainaiva gacchāmi, gāmaisu nigamaisu ca||

220.

daṇḍahatthā  nivārainti, itthiyau purisā ca maṃ|

auk‍kitā aukiṇṇā (sī॰) pūtigandhaina, māssu auraina āgamā māgamā (sī॰ pī॰)||

221.

aitādisaṃ idaṃ dukkhaṃ, satta vassāni dāni mai|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai duk‍kaṭamattanau||

222.

taṃ  vau vadāmi bhaddantai bhaddaṃ vau (sī॰ pī॰), yāvantaittha samāgatā|

māssu mittāna mittānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) dubbhitthau, mittadubbhau hi pāpakau||

223.

kuṭṭhī kilāsī bhavati, yau mittānidha dubbhati yau mittānaṃ idhaddubhi (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), yau mittānaṃ idha dubbhati (pī॰)|

kāyassa bhaidā mittaddu mittadubbhī (syā॰ ka॰), nirayaṃ saupapaj‍jatīti sau upapaj‍jatīti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

mahākapijātakaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

517. dakarakkhasajātakaṃ (7)

224.

sacai vau vuyhamānānaṃ, sattan‍naṃ udakaṇṇavai|

manussabalimaisānau, nāvaṃ gaṇhaiyya rakkhasau|

anupubbaṃ kathaṃ datvā, muñ‍caisi dakarakkhasā dakarakkhatau (pī॰)||

225.

mātaraṃ paṭhamaṃ daj‍jaṃ, bhariyaṃ datvāna bhātaraṃ|

tatau sahāyaṃ datvāna, pañ‍camaṃ daj‍jaṃ daj‍ja (syā॰) brāhmaṇaṃ|

chaṭṭhāhaṃ daj‍jamattānaṃ, naiva daj‍jaṃ mahausadhaṃ||

226.

pausaitā tai janaittī ca, dīgharattānukampikā|

chabbhī tayi padussati paduṭṭhasmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰), paṇḍitā atthadassinī|

añ‍ñaṃ  upanisaṃ katvā, vadhā taṃ parimaucayi||

227.

taṃ tādisiṃ tādisaṃ (ka॰) pāṇadadiṃ, aurasaṃ gabbhadhāriniṃ gabbhadhāriṇiṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

mātaraṃ kaina dausaina, daj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau dakarakkhatau (pī॰)||

228.

daharā viyalaṅkāraṃ, dhāraiti apiḷandhanaṃ|

dauvārikai anīkaṭṭhai, ativailaṃ pajagghati sañ‍jagghati (ka॰)||

229.

athaupi  paṭirājūnaṃ, sayaṃ dūtāni sāsati|

mātaraṃ taina dausaina, daj‍jāhaṃ dakarakkhinau||

230.

itthigumbassa pavarā, ac‍cantaṃ piyabhāṇinī ac‍cantapiyavādinī (sī॰ pī॰)|

anuggatā anubbatā (syā॰), anupubbatā (ka॰) sīlavatī, chāyāva anapāyinī||

231.

ak‍kaudhanā puñ‍ñavatī pañ‍ñavatī (sī॰ pī॰), paṇḍitā atthadassinī|

ubbariṃ uppariṃ (sī॰) kaina dausaina, daj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau||

232.

khiḍḍāratisamāpan‍naṃ, anatthavasamāgataṃ|

sā maṃ sakāna puttānaṃ, ayācaṃ yācatai dhanaṃ||

233.

sauhaṃ  dadāmi sārattau sāratau (syā॰), bahuṃ uc‍cāvacaṃ dhanaṃ|

suduc‍cajaṃ cajitvāna, pacchā saucāmi dummanau|

ubbariṃ taina dausaina, daj‍jāhaṃ dakarakkhinau||

234.

yainaucitā janapadā jānapadā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ānītā ca paṭiggahaṃ|

ābhataṃ pararaj‍jaibhi, abhiṭṭhāya bahuṃ dhanaṃ||

235.

dhanuggahānaṃ pavaraṃ, sūraṃ tikhiṇamantinaṃ|

bhātaraṃ kaina dausaina, daj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau||

236.

yainaucitā  mayaucitā (sī॰), mayācitā (pī॰) janapadā, ānītā ca paṭiggahaṃ|

ābhataṃ pararaj‍jaibhi, abhiṭṭhāya bahuṃ dhanaṃ||

237.

dhanuggahānaṃ pavarau, sūrau tikhiṇamanti ca tikhiṇamantinau (ka॰)|

mayāyaṃ mayā sau (sī॰ pī॰) sukhitau rājā, atimañ‍ñati dārakau||

238.

upaṭṭhānampi mai ayyai, na sau aiti yathā purai|

bhātaraṃ taina dausaina, daj‍jāhaṃ dakarakkhinau||

239.

aikarattaina ubhayau, tvañ‍caiva dhanusaikha ca dhanusaikhavā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

ubhau jātaittha pañ‍cālā, sahāyā susamāvayā||

240.

cariyā  taṃ anubandhitthau anubandhau (ka॰), aikadukkhasukhau tava|

ussuk‍kau tai divārattiṃ, sabbakic‍caisu byāvaṭau|

sahāyaṃ kaina dausaina, daj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau||

241.

cariyā maṃ ayaṃ cariyāya ayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) ayyai, pajagghitthau sañ‍jagghitthau (syā॰) mayā saha|

aj‍jāpi taina vaṇṇaina, ativailaṃ pajagghati||

242.

ubbariyāpihaṃ ayyai, mantayāmi rahaugatau|

anāmantau anāmantā (sī॰) pavisati, pubbai appaṭivaiditau||

243.

laddhadvārau laddhavārau (sī॰ pī॰) kataukāsau, ahirikaṃ anādaraṃ|

sahāyaṃ taina dausaina, daj‍jāhaṃ dakarakkhinau||

244.

kusalau sabbanimittānaṃ, rutañ‍ñū rudañ‍ñū (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) āgatāgamau|

uppātai supinai yuttau, niyyānai ca pavaisanai||

245.

paṭṭhau paddhau (sī॰ pī॰) bhūmantalikkhasmiṃ, nakkhattapadakauvidau|

brāhmaṇaṃ  kaina dausaina, daj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau||

246.

parisāyampi mai ayyai, ummīlitvā udikkhati|

tasmā ac‍cabhamuṃ luddaṃ, daj‍jāhaṃ dakarakkhinau||

247.

sasamuddapariyāyaṃ, mahiṃ sāgarakuṇḍalaṃ|

vasundharaṃ āvasasi, amac‍caparivāritau||

248.

cāturantau mahāraṭṭhau, vijitāvī mahabbalau|

pathabyā aikarājāsi, yasau tai vipulaṃ gatau||

249.

sauḷasitthisahassāni, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā|

nānājanapadā nārī, daivakañ‍ñūpamā subhā||

250.

aivaṃ sabbaṅgasampan‍naṃ, sabbakāmasamiddhinaṃ|

sukhitānaṃ piyaṃ dīghaṃ, jīvitaṃ āhu khattiya||

251.

atha tvaṃ kaina vaṇṇaina, kaina vā pana haitunā|

paṇḍitaṃ anurakkhantau, pāṇaṃ cajasi duc‍cajaṃ||

252.

yataupi  āgatau ayyai, mama hatthaṃ mahausadhau|

nābhijānāmi dhīrassa, anumattampi duk‍kaṭaṃ||

253.

sacai ca kismici kālai, maraṇaṃ mai purai siyā|

sau mai puttai puttai ca mai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ paputtai ca, sukhāpaiyya mahausadhau||

254.

anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ, sabbamatthampi passati sabbamatthaṃ vipassati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

anāparādhakammantaṃ, na daj‍jaṃ dakarakkhinau||

255.

idaṃ suṇātha pañ‍cālā, cūḷanaiyyassa cūḷanīyassa (sī॰) bhāsitaṃ|

paṇḍitaṃ  anurakkhantau, pāṇaṃ cajati duc‍cajaṃ||

256.

mātu bhariyāya bhātuc‍ca, sakhinau brāhmaṇassa ca|

attanau cāpi pañ‍cālau, chan‍naṃ cajati jīvitaṃ||

257.

aivaṃ mahatthikā khahiddhiyā (syā॰), mahiddhikā (ka॰) pañ‍ñā, nipuṇā sādhucintinī|

diṭṭhadhammahitatthāya, samparāyasukhāya cāti||

dakarakkhasajātakaṃ sattamaṃ|

518. paṇḍaranāgarājajātakaṃ (8)

258.

vikiṇṇavācaṃ aniguyha anigūḷha (pī॰) mantaṃ, asañ‍ñataṃ aparicakkhitāraṃ aparirakkhitāraṃ (ka॰)|

bhayaṃ tamanvaiti sayaṃ abaudhaṃ, nāgaṃ yathā paṇḍarakaṃ supaṇṇau suvaṇṇau (ka॰)||

259.

yau guyhamantaṃ parirakkhanaiyyaṃ, mauhā narau saṃsati hāsamānau bhāsamānau (pī॰)|

taṃ bhin‍namantaṃ bhayamanvaiti khippaṃ, nāgaṃ yathā paṇḍarakaṃ supaṇṇau||

260.

nānumittau  garuṃ atthaṃ, guyhaṃ vaiditumarahati|

sumittau ca asambuddhaṃ, sambuddhaṃ vā anattha vā||

261.

vissāsamāpaj‍jimahaṃ  acailaṃ acailau (sī॰ pī॰), samaṇau  ayaṃ sammatau bhāvitattau|

tassāhamakkhiṃ vivariṃ vivaraṃ (sī॰) guyhamatthaṃ, atītamatthau kapaṇaṃ kapaṇau (pī॰) rudāmi||

262.

tassāhaṃ paramaṃ purimaṃ (sī॰) brahmai guyhaṃ, vācañhi maṃ nāsakkhiṃ nāsakkhi (pī॰) saṃyamaituṃ|

tappakkhatau hi bhayamāgataṃ mamaṃ, atītamatthau kapaṇaṃ rudāmi||

263.

yau vai narau suhadaṃ mañ‍ñamānau, guyhamatthaṃ saṃsati duk‍kulīnai|

dausā bhayā athavā rāgarattā rāgarattau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), pal‍latthitau pal‍litthau (pī॰), pal‍lattitau (ka॰) bālau asaṃsayaṃ sau||

264.

tiraukkhavācau asataṃ paviṭṭhau, yau saṅgatīsu mudīraiti vākyaṃ|

āsīvisau dummukhautyāhu taṃ naraṃ, ārā ārā ārā arā (pī॰) saṃyamai tādisamhā||

265.

an‍naṃ pānaṃ kāsika kāsikaṃ (pī॰) candanañ‍ca, manāpitthiyau mālamucchādanañ‍ca|

auhāya gacchāmasai sabbakāmai, supaṇṇa pāṇūpagatāva tyamhā||

266.

kau nīdha tiṇṇaṃ garahaṃ upaiti, asmiṃdha  laukai pāṇabhū nāgarāja|

samaṇau supaṇṇau atha vā tvamaiva, kiṃ kāraṇā paṇḍarakaggahītau||

267.

samaṇauti  mai sammatattau ahausi, piyau ca mai manasā bhāvitattau|

tassāhamakkhiṃ vivariṃ guyhamatthaṃ, atītamatthau kapaṇaṃ rudāmi||

268.

na catthi sattau amarau pathabyā, pañ‍ñāvidhā natthi na ninditabbā|

sac‍caina dhammaina dhitiyā dhiyā (sī॰ pī॰) damaina, alabbhamabyāharatī narau idha||

269.

mātāpitā paramā bandhavānaṃ, nāssa tatiyau anukampakatthi|

taisampi guyhaṃ paramaṃ na saṃsai, mantassa bhaidaṃ parisaṅkamānau||

270.

mātāpitā bhaginī bhātarau ca, sahāyā vā yassa haunti sapakkhā|

taisampi guyhaṃ paramaṃ na saṃsai, mantassa bhaidaṃ parisaṅkamānau||

271.

bhariyā cai purisaṃ vaj‍jā, kaumārī piyabhāṇinī|

puttarūpayasūpaitā, ñātisaṅghapurakkhatā, tassāpi guyhaṃ paramaṃ na saṃsai|

mantassa bhaidaṃ parisaṅkamānau||

272.

na guyhamatthaṃ guyhatthaṃ (ka॰) vivaraiyya, rakkhaiyya naṃ yathā nidhiṃ|

na hi pātukatau sādhu, guyhau atthau pajānatā||

273.

thiyā guyhaṃ na saṃsaiyya, amittassa ca paṇḍitau|

yau cāmisaina saṃhīrau, hadayatthainau ca yau narau||

274.

guyhamatthaṃ  asambuddhaṃ, sambaudhayati yau narau|

mantabhaidabhayā tassa, dāsabhūtau titikkhati||

275.

yāvantau  purisassatthaṃ, guyhaṃ jānanti mantinaṃ|

tāvantau tassa ubbaigā, tasmā guyhaṃ na vissajai||

276.

vivic‍ca bhāsaiyya divā rahassaṃ, rattiṃ giraṃ nātivailaṃ pamuñ‍cai|

upassutikā hi suṇanti mantaṃ, tasmā mantau khippamupaiti bhaidaṃ||

277.

yathāpi assa ayau (syā॰), aya (ka॰) nagaraṃ mahantaṃ, advārakaṃ āḷārakaṃ (pī॰) āyasaṃ bhaddasālaṃ|

samantakhātāparikhāupaitaṃ, aivampi mai tai idha guyhamantā||

278.

yai guyhamantā avikiṇṇavācā, daḷhā  sadatthaisu narā dujivha dujivhā (syā॰ pī॰)|

ārā amittā byavajanti taihi, āsīvisā vā riva sattusaṅghā sattasaṅghā (sī॰ syā॰)||

279.

hitvā gharaṃ pabbajitau acailau, naggau muṇḍau carati ghāsahaitu|

tamhi tamhī (pī॰) nu khau vivariṃ guyhamatthaṃ, atthā ca dhammā ca apaggatamhā apāgatamhā (sī॰), apagatamhā (syā॰), avāgatamhā (pī॰)||

280.

kathaṃkarau hauti supaṇṇarāja, kiṃsīlau kaina vataina vattaṃ|

samaṇau caraṃ hitvā mamāyitāni, kathaṃkarau saggamupaiti ṭhānaṃ||

281.

hiriyā titikkhāya damainupaitau damaina khantiyā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ak‍kaudhanau paisuṇiyaṃ pahāya|

samaṇau caraṃ hitvā mamāyitāni, aivaṃkarau saggamupaiti ṭhānaṃ||

282.

mātāva  puttaṃ taruṇaṃ tanuj‍jaṃ tanūjaṃ (sī॰), samphassatā sampassa taṃ (sī॰ pī॰) sabbagattaṃ pharaiti|

aivampi mai tvaṃ pāturahu dijinda, mātāva puttaṃ anukampamānau||

283.

handaj‍ja  tvaṃ muñ‍ca muc‍ca (sī॰) vadhā dujivha, tayau hi puttā na hi añ‍ñau atthi|

antaivāsī din‍nakau atrajau ca, raj‍jassu rajassu (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) puttañ‍ñatarau mai ahausi||

284.

ic‍caiva vākyaṃ visaj‍jī supaṇṇau, bhumyaṃ patiṭṭhāya dijau dujivhaṃ|

muttaj‍ja tvaṃ sabbabhayātivattau, thalūdakai hauhi mayābhiguttau||

285.

ātaṅkinaṃ yathā kusalau bhisak‍kau, pipāsitānaṃ rahadauva sītau|

vaismaṃ yathā himasītaṭṭitānaṃ himasisiraṭṭitānaṃ (pī॰), aivampi tai saraṇamahaṃ bhavāmi||

286.

sandhiṃ katvā amittaina, aṇḍajaina jalābuja|

vivariya dāṭhaṃ saisi, kutau taṃ bhayamāgataṃ||

287.

saṅkaithaiva amittasmiṃ, mittasmimpi na vissasai|

abhayā bhayamuppan‍naṃ, api mūlāni kantati||

288.

kathaṃ nu vissasai tyamhi, yaināsi kalahau katau|

nic‍cayattaina ṭhātabbaṃ, sau disabbhi sau disamhi (pī॰) na raj‍jati||

289.

vissāsayai  na ca taṃ naṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vissayaiyya, asaṅkitau saṅkitau ca asaṅkitau ca saṅkitau (sī॰ pī॰) bhavaiyya|

tathā  tathā viñ‍ñū parak‍kamaiyya, yathā yathā bhāvaṃ parau na jañ‍ñā||

290.

tai  daivavaṇṇā daivavaṇṇī (pī॰) sukhumālarūpā, ubhau samā sujayā sujayau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) puñ‍ñakhandhā puñ‍ñagandhā (pī॰)|

upāgamuṃ karampiyaṃ kādambiyaṃ (sī॰), kārambiyaṃ (pī॰) acailaṃ, missībhūtā assavāhāva nāgā||

291.

tatau havai paṇḍarakau acailaṃ, sayamaivupāgamma idaṃ avauca|

muttaj‍jahaṃ sabbabhayātivattau, na hi ha (pī॰) nūna tuyhaṃ manasau piyamhā||

292.

piyau hi mai āsi supaṇṇarājā, asaṃsayaṃ paṇḍarakaina sac‍caṃ|

sau rāgarattauva akāsimaitaṃ, pāpakammaṃ pāpaṃ kammaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) sampajānau na mauhā||

293.

na mai piyaṃ appiyaṃ vāpi hauti, sampassatau laukamimaṃ parañ‍ca|

susañ‍ñatānañhi viyañ‍janaina, asañ‍ñatau laukamimaṃ carāsi||

294.

ariyāvakāsausi anariyauvāsi anariyau cāsi (sī॰ pī॰), asañ‍ñatau sañ‍ñatasan‍nikāsau|

kaṇhābhijātikausi  anariyarūpau, pāpaṃ bahuṃ duc‍caritaṃ acāri||

295.

aduṭṭhassa tuvaṃ dubbhi, dubbhī dūbhi, dūbhī (pī॰) ca pisuṇau casi|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, muddhā tai phalatu sattadhā||

296.

tasmā hi mittānaṃ na dubbhitabbaṃ, mittadubbhā mittadubbhā hi (syā॰) pāpiyau natthi añ‍ñau|

āsittasattau nihatau pathabyā, indassa vākyaina hi saṃvarau hatauti||

paṇḍaranāgarājajātakaṃ paṇḍarakajātakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aṭṭhamaṃ|

519. sambulājātakaṃ (9)

297.

kā  vaidhamānā girikandarāyaṃ, aikā tuvaṃ tiṭṭhasi saṃhitūru sañ‍ñatūra (sī॰ pī॰), sañ‍citūru (syā॰)|

puṭṭhāsi mai pāṇipamaiyyamajjhai, akkhāhi mai nāmañ‍ca bandhavai ca||

298.

aubhāsayaṃ vanaṃ rammaṃ, sīhabyagghanisaivitaṃ|

kā vā tvamasi kalyāṇi, kassa vā tvaṃ sumajjhimai|

abhivādaimi taṃ bhaddai, dānavāhaṃ namatthu tai||

299.

yau puttau kāsirājassa, sautthisainauti taṃ vidū|

tassāhaṃ sambulā bhariyā, aivaṃ jānāhi dānava|

abhivādaimi  taṃ bhantai bhaddantai (sī॰ syā॰), sambulāhaṃ namatthu tai||

300.

vaidaihaputtau  bhaddantai, vanai vasati āturau|

tamahaṃ raugasammattaṃ, aikā aikaṃ upaṭṭhahaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ (sī॰)||

301.

ahañ‍ca vanamuñchāya, madhumaṃsaṃ migābilaṃ migāvilaṃ (sī॰)|

yadā harāmi taṃ bhakkhau, tassa nūnaj‍ja nādhati nāthati (ka॰)||

302.

kiṃ vanai rājaputtaina, āturaina karissasi|

sambulai pariciṇṇaina, ahaṃ bhattā bhavāmi tai||

303.

saukaṭṭāya durattāya, kiṃ rūpaṃ vij‍jatai mama|

añ‍ñaṃ pariyaisa bhaddantai, abhirūpataraṃ mayā||

304.

aihimaṃ girimāruyha, bhariyā mai mayhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) catussatā|

tāsaṃ tvaṃ pavarā hauhi, sabbakāmasamiddhinī||

305.

nūna nanu (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) tārakavaṇṇābhai hāṭakavaṇṇābhai (pī॰), yaṃ kiñ‍ci manasicchasi|

sabbaṃ taṃ pacuraṃ mayhaṃ, ramassvaj‍ja ramasuj‍ja (sī॰ syā॰) mayā saha||

306.

nau cai tuvaṃ mahaisaiyyaṃ, sambulai kārayissasi|

alaṃ tvaṃ pātarāsāya, paṇhai mañ‍ñai (sī॰ pī॰) bhakkhā bhavissasi||

307.

tañ‍ca  sattajaṭau luddau, kaḷārau purisādakau|

vanai nāthaṃ apassantiṃ, sambulaṃ aggahī bhujai||

308.

adhipan‍nā pisācaina, luddaināmisacakkhunā|

sā ca sattuvasampattā, patimaivānusaucati||

309.

na mai idaṃ tathā dukkhaṃ, yaṃ maṃ khādaiyya rakkhasau|

yañ‍ca  mai ayyaputtassa, manau haissati añ‍ñathā||

310.

na santi daivā pavasanti nūna, na hi nūna santi idha laukapālā|

sahasā karauntānamasañ‍ñatānaṃ, na hi nūna santi paṭisaidhitārau||

311.

itthīnamaisā pavarā yasassinī, santā samā aggirivuggataijā|

tañ‍cai tuvaṃ rakkhasādaisi kañ‍ñaṃ, muddhā ca hi sattadhā tai phalaiyya|

mā tvaṃ dahī jahī (pī॰) muñ‍ca patibbatāya patibbatā sā (sī॰), patibbatā yā (pī॰)||

312.

sā ca assamamāgacchi, pamuttā purisādakā|

nīḷaṃ niḍḍaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) paḷinaṃ sakuṇīva phalinasakuṇīva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), gatasiṅgaṃva ālayaṃ||

313.

sā tattha paridaivaisi, rājaputtī yasassinī|

sambulā utumattakkhā, vanai nāthaṃ apassantī apassatī (sī॰)||

314.

samaṇai  brāhmaṇai vandai, sampan‍nacaraṇai isai|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi tumhaṃ hi (pī॰) saraṇaṃ gatā||

315.

vandai sīhai ca byagghai ca, yai ca añ‍ñai vanai migā|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi saraṇaṃ gatā||

316.

tiṇā tiṇa (pī॰) latāni ausadhyau, pabbatāni vanāni ca|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi saraṇaṃ gatā||

317.

vandai  indīvarīsāmaṃ, rattiṃ nakkhattamāliniṃ|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi saraṇaṃ gatā||

318.

vandai bhāgīrathiṃ gaṅgaṃ, savantīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi saraṇaṃ gatā||

319.

vandai ahaṃ pabbatarājasaiṭṭhaṃ, himavantaṃ siluc‍cayaṃ|

rājaputtaṃ apassantī, tumhaṃmhi saraṇaṃ gatā||

320.

atisāyaṃ vatāgacchi, rājaputti yasassini|

kaina nuj‍ja samāgacchi samāgacchi (sī॰ pī॰), kau tai piyatarau mayā||

321.

idaṃ khauhaṃ tadāvaucaṃ tadavaucaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), gahitā taina sattunā|

na mai idaṃ tathā dukkhaṃ, yaṃ maṃ khādaiyya rakkhasau|

yañ‍ca mai ayyaputtassa, manau haissati añ‍ñathā||

322.

caurīnaṃ bahubuddhīnaṃ, yāsu sac‍caṃ sudul‍labhaṃ|

thīnaṃ bhāvau durājānau, macchassaivaudakai gataṃ||

323.

tathā maṃ sac‍caṃ pālaitu, pālayissati cai mamaṃ|

yathāhaṃ nābhijānāmi, añ‍ñaṃ piyataraṃ tayā|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, byādhi tai vūpasammatu||

324.

yai kuñ‍jarā sattasatā uḷārā, rakkhanti rattindivamuyyutāvudhā|

dhanuggahānañ‍ca satāni sauḷasa, kathaṃvidhai passasi bhaddai sattavau||

325.

alaṅkatāyau  padumuttarattacā, virāgitā passati haṃsagaggarā|

tāsaṃ suṇitvā mitagītavāditaṃ mitagītavādinaṃ (pī॰), na dāni mai tāta tathā yathā purai||

326.

suvaṇṇasaṃkac‍cadharā  suviggahā, alaṅkatā mānusiyaccharūpamā|

sainaupiyā sainūpiyā (pī॰) tāta aninditaṅgiyau, khattiyakañ‍ñā paṭilaubhayanti paṭilābhayanti (pī॰) naṃ||

327.

sacai ahaṃ tāta tathā yathā purai, patiṃ tamuñchāya punā vanai bharai|

sammānayai maṃ na ca maṃ vimānayai, itaupi mai tāta tatau varaṃ siyā||

328.

yaman‍napānai  vipulasmi auhitai, nārī vimaṭṭhābharaṇā alaṅkatā|

sabbaṅgupaitā pañ‍caṅgupaitā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) patinau ca appiyā, abajjha avajjha (syā॰), ābajjha (pī॰) tassā maraṇaṃ tatau varaṃ||

329.

api cai daliddā kapaṇā anāḷhiyā, kaṭādutīyā patinau ca sā piyā|

sabbaṅgupaitāyapi appiyāya, ayamaiva  saiyyā saiyyau (syā॰ ka॰) kapaṇāpi yā piyā kapaṇāpi yā (ka॰)||

330.

sudul‍labhitthī purisassa yā hitā, bhattitthiyā dul‍labhau yau hitau ca|

hitā ca tai sīlavatī ca bhariyā, janinda dhammaṃ cara sambulāya||

331.

sacai tuvaṃ vipulai laddhabhaugai, issāvatiṇṇā maraṇaṃ upaisi|

ahañ‍ca tai bhaddai imā rājakañ‍ñā imā ca kañ‍ñā (pī॰), sabbai sabbaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tai vacanakarā bhavāmāti||

sambulājātakaṃ navamaṃ|

520. gandhatindukajātakaṃ (10)

332.

appamādau  amataṃ padaṃ amatapadaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), pamādau mac‍cunau padaṃ|

appamattā na mīyanti, yai pamattā yathā matā||

333.

madā pamādau jāyaitha, pamādā jāyatai khayau|

khayā padausā khayā ca dausā (sī॰) jāyanti, mā pamādau mā madau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bharatūsabha bhāradhūsabha (ka॰)||

334.

bahū hi khattiyā jīnā, atthaṃ raṭṭhaṃ pamādinau|

athaupi gāminau gāmā, anagārā agārinau||

335.

khattiyassa pamattassa, raṭṭhasmiṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhana|

sabbai bhaugā vinassanti, rañ‍ñau taṃ vuc‍catai aghaṃ||

336.

naisa  dhammau mahārāja, ativailaṃ pamaj‍jasi|

iddhaṃ phītaṃ janapadaṃ, caurā viddhaṃsayanti naṃ||

337.

na tai puttā bhavissanti, na hirañ‍ñaṃ na dhāniyaṃ na hirañ‍ñanidhāniyā (ka॰)|

raṭṭhai viluppamānamhi, sabbabhaugaihi jiyyasi||

338.

sabbabhaugā parijiṇṇaṃ, rājānaṃ vāpi khattiyaṃ khattiya (ka॰)|

ñātimittā suhaj‍jā ca, na taṃ mañ‍ñanti māniyaṃ mantiyaṃ (syā॰)||

339.

hatthārauhā anīkaṭṭhā, rathikā pattikārakā|

tamaivamupajīvantā, na taṃ mañ‍ñanti māniyaṃ||

340.

asaṃvihitakammantaṃ, bālaṃ dummantimantinaṃ|

sirī jahati dummaidhaṃ, jiṇṇaṃva uragau tacaṃ||

341.

susaṃvihitakammantaṃ, kāluṭṭhāyiṃ atanditaṃ|

sabbai bhaugābhivaḍḍhanti, gāvau sausabhāmiva||

342.

upassutiṃ mahārāja, raṭṭhai janapadai cara|

tattha disvā ca sutvā ca, tatau taṃ tvaṃ (ka॰) paṭipaj‍jasi||

343.

aivaṃ vaidaitu pañ‍cālau, saṅgāmai saramappitau samappitau (sī॰ pī॰)|

yathāhamaj‍ja vaidaimi, kaṇṭakaina samappitau||

344.

jiṇṇau  dubbalacakkhūsi, na rūpaṃ sādhu passasi|

kiṃ tattha brahmadattassa, yaṃ taṃ maggaiyya magghaiyya (pī॰) kaṇṭakau kaṇḍakau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

345.

bahvaittha brahmadattassa, sauhaṃ yauhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) maggasmi maggausmi (pī॰) brāhmaṇa|

arakkhitā  jānapadā, adhammabalinā hatā||

346.

rattiñhi rattiñ‍ca (sī॰) caurā khādanti, divā khādanti tuṇḍiyā|

raṭṭhasmiṃ kūṭarājassa, bahu adhammikau janau||

347.

aitādisai bhayai jātai tāta (sī॰ pī॰), bhayaṭṭā tāta tāva (sī॰ pī॰) māṇavā|

nil‍lainakāni kubbanti, vanai āhatva kaṇṭakaṃ||

348.

kadāssu nāmayaṃ rājā, brahmadattau marissati|

yassa raṭṭhamhi jiyyanti, appatikā kumārikā||

349.

dubbhāsitañhi tai jammi, anatthapadakauvidai|

kuhiṃ rājā kumārīnaṃ, bhattāraṃ pariyaisati||

350.

na mai dubbhāsitaṃ brahmai, kauvidatthapadā ahaṃ|

arakkhitā jānapadā, adhammabalinā hatā||

351.

rattiñhi  caurā khādanti, divā khādanti tuṇḍiyā|

raṭṭhasmiṃ kūṭarājassa, bahu adhammikau janau|

duj‍jīvai dubbharai dārai, kutau bhattā kumāriyau||

352.

aivaṃ sayatu pañ‍cālau, saṅgāmai sattiyā hatau|

yathāyaṃ kapaṇau saiti, hatau phālaina sāliyau||

353.

adhammaina tuvaṃ jamma, brahmadattassa kujjhasi|

yau tvaṃ sapasi rājānaṃ, aparajjhitvāna attanau attanā (pī॰)||

354.

dhammaina brahmadattassa, ahaṃ kujjhāmi brāhmaṇa|

arakkhitā jānapadā, adhammabalinā hatā||

355.

rattiñhi  caurā khādanti, divā khādanti tuṇḍiyā|

raṭṭhasmiṃ kūṭarājassa, bahu adhammikau janau||

356.

sā  nūna puna rai pak‍kā, vikālai bhattamāhari|

bhattahāriṃ apaikkhantau, hatau phālaina sāliyau||

357.

aivaṃ hañ‍ñatu pañ‍cālau, saṅgāmai asinā hatau daḷhaṃ (pī॰)|

yathāhamaj‍ja pahatau, khīrañ‍ca mai pavaṭṭitaṃ||

358.

yaṃ pasu khīraṃ chaḍḍaiti, pasupālaṃ vihiṃsati pasupālañ‍ca hiṃsati (sī॰), pasu phālañ‍ca hiṃsati (pī॰)|

kiṃ tattha brahmadattassa, yaṃ nau garahatai garahatau (pī॰) bhavaṃ||

359.

gārayhau brahmai pañ‍cālau, brahmadattassa rājinau|

arakkhitā jānapadā, adhammabalinā hatā||

360.

rattiñhi  caurā khādanti, divā khādanti tuṇḍiyā|

raṭṭhasmiṃ kūṭarājassa, bahu adhammikau janau||

361.

caṇḍā aṭanakā aṭanaka (pī॰), akaṭanā (ka॰) gāvī, yaṃ purai na duhāmasai|

taṃ dāni aj‍ja dauhāma, khīrakāmaihupaddutā||

362.

aivaṃ kandatu pañ‍cālau, viputtau vippasukkhatu|

yathāyaṃ kapaṇā gāvī, viputtā paridhāvati||

363.

yaṃ pasu pasupālassa, sambhamaiyya pabbhamaiyya (sī॰ pī॰) ravaiyya vā|

kau nīdha aparādhatthi, brahmadattassa rājinau||

364.

aparādhau mahābrahmai, brahmadattassa rājinau|

arakkhitā jānapadā, adhammabalinā hatā||

365.

rattiñhi caurā khādanti, divā khādanti tuṇḍiyā|

raṭṭhasmiṃ kūṭarājassa, bahu adhammikau janau|

kathaṃ nau asikausatthā, khīrapā hañ‍ñatai pajā||

366.

aivaṃ khaj‍jatu pañ‍cālau, hatau yuddhai saputtakau|

yathāhamaj‍ja khaj‍jāmi, gāmikaihi gāmakaihi (sī॰ pī॰) arañ‍ñajau||

367.

na  sabbabhūtaisu vidhainti vidhanti (ka॰) rakkhaṃ, rājānau maṇḍūka manussalaukai|

naittāvatā rājā adhammacārī, yaṃ tādisaṃ jīvamadaiyyu dhaṅkā||

368.

adhammarūpau vata brahmacārī, anuppiyaṃ bhāsasi khattiyassa|

viluppamānāya puthuppajāya, pūjaisi rājaṃ paramappamādaṃ rājaṃ paramappavādaṃ (sī॰), rājā paramappavādiṃ (syā॰)||

369.

sacai  imaṃ brahmai suraj‍jakaṃ siyā, phītaṃ raṭṭhaṃ muditaṃ pūritaṃ (ka॰) vippasan‍naṃ|

bhutvā baliṃ aggapiṇḍañ‍ca kākā, na mādisaṃ jīvamadaiyyu dhaṅkāti||

gandhatindukajātakaṃ dasamaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

kiṃchanda  kumbha jayaddisa chaddanta, atha paṇḍitasambhava sirakapi|

dakarakkhasa paṇḍaranāgavarau, atha sambula tindukadaivasutauti||

tiṃsanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


jātakapāḷiyā paṭhamau bhāgau niṭṭhitau|
